    PROCEDURE insertlog@301510000(pNo@301510000 : Code[50]);
    VAR
      lTempLogLACK@301510001 : Record 50001;
      entryno@301510002 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      entryno := 1;
      IF lTempLogLACK.FINDLAST THEN
        entryno += lTempLogLACK."Entry No.";

      "Entry No." := entryno;
      user := USERID;
      date := CURRENTDATETIME;
      printeddocument := pNo;
      IF INSERT THEN;
    END;

      // 00 15.02.19 LACK only Debug

    END.

                                                                  DimMgt.TypeToTableID1("Bal. Account Type"),"Bal. Account No.",
                                                                  DATABASE::Job,"Job No.",

                                                                  DATABASE::Campaign,"Campaign No.",0,'',0,'',0,'');

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckEntry@301490000();
    VAR
      ExtInterfaceSetup@301490000 : Record 5275373;
    BEGIN
      // 01 01.06.21 MWAN MSD-15850 Redesign Function "CheckPreviousEntry"->CheckEntry
      ExtInterfaceSetup.GET("Ext. Interface Setup Code");
      ExtInterfaceSetup.TESTFIELD("Retry Attempt",TRUE);
      IF "Attempt No." <=0 THEN
        ERROR(Text003,Rec.FIELDCAPTION("Attempt No."),"Attempt No.");
      IF ExtInterfaceSetup."Max. Number of Attempts" < "Attempt No." THEN
        ERROR(Text002,ExtInterfaceSetup."Max. Number of Attempts");
      // 01 01.06.21 MWAN MSD-15850 Redesign Function "CheckPreviousEntry"->CheckEntry END
      IF NOT ExtInterfaceAttemptTimeout.GET("Ext. Interface Setup Code","Attempt No."-1) AND ( "Attempt No." > 1)  THEN
        ERROR(Text001,"Attempt No.","Ext. Interface Setup Code","Attempt No."-1);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      // 00 25.05.21 MWAN MSD-15850 Created
      // 01 01.06.21 MWAN MSD-15850 New Function "CheckPreviousEntry"
      // 01 07.06.21 MWAN MSD-15850 Redesign Function "CheckPreviousEntry"->CheckEntry
      // 01 08.06.21 MWAN MSD-15850 Set Min Value and Init Value for "Waiting Time", Set Min Value for "Attempt No."
    }
    END.
  }

    PROCEDURE SetEntryNo@301490000() : Integer;
    VAR
      PaygateEntry@301510000 : Record 50009;
    BEGIN
      PaygateEntry.RESET;
      IF PaygateEntry.FIND('+') THEN
        EXIT(PaygateEntry."Entry No."+1)
      ELSE
        EXIT(1);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetEntryNoDoc@301490001(DocNo@301490001 : Code[20]) : Integer;
    VAR
      PaygateEntry@301510000 : Record 50009;
    BEGIN
      PaygateEntry.RESET;
      PaygateEntry.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      IF PaygateEntry.FIND('+') THEN
        EXIT(PaygateEntry."Document Position"+1)
      ELSE
        EXIT(1);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateLines@301510000();
    VAR
      PaygateEntry@301510000 : Record 50009;
      PaygateEntry1@301510001 : Record 50009;
      LineCount@301510002 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      PaygateEntry.RESET;
      PaygateEntry.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
      PaygateEntry.SETFILTER("Entry No.",'>=%1',"Entry No.");
      IF PaygateEntry.FIND('-') THEN REPEAT
        PaygateEntry1.SETRANGE("Document No.",PaygateEntry."Document No.");
        PaygateEntry1.SETFILTER("Entry No.",'>%1',PaygateEntry."Entry No.");
        IF PaygateEntry1.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
          IF LineCount = 0 THEN BEGIN
            PaygateEntry1.Residual:=Residual-PaygateEntry1."Request Amount";
            LineCount+=1;
          END ELSE
            PaygateEntry1.Residual:=PaygateEntry.Residual-PaygateEntry1."Request Amount";
          PaygateEntry1.MODIFY;
        END;
      UNTIL PaygateEntry.NEXT=0;
      COMMIT;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      //00 03.09.21 NKON CHRON-2 Tab. angelegt mehr. Zahlungen fÅr PICKUP
    }
    END.
  }
}

    PROCEDURE CreateJsonObjectOrderStatus@301510000(OSL@301480000 : Record 50010) : Text;
    VAR
      JSONManagement@301480001 : Codeunit 5459;
      Contact@301480007 : Record 5050;
      JObject@301480002 : DotNet "'Newtonsoft.Json, Version=9.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=30ad4fe6b2a6aeed'.Newtonsoft.Json.Linq.JObject";
      JObjectInvoiceAddress@301480008 : DotNet "'Newtonsoft.Json, Version=9.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=30ad4fe6b2a6aeed'.Newtonsoft.Json.Linq.JObject";
      JObjectDeliveryAddress@301480009 : DotNet "'Newtonsoft.Json, Version=9.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=30ad4fe6b2a6aeed'.Newtonsoft.Json.Linq.JObject";
      JObjectBuergelInvoiceAddress@301480010 : DotNet "'Newtonsoft.Json, Version=9.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=30ad4fe6b2a6aeed'.Newtonsoft.Json.Linq.JObject";
      JObjectBuergelDeliveryAddress@301480011 : DotNet "'Newtonsoft.Json, Version=9.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=30ad4fe6b2a6aeed'.Newtonsoft.Json.Linq.JObject";
      JArrayOrderPositions@301480004 : DotNet "'Newtonsoft.Json'.Newtonsoft.Json.Linq.JArray";
      JArrayOrderPromotions@301480013 : DotNet "'Newtonsoft.Json'.Newtonsoft.Json.Linq.JArray";
      orderAmount@301480003 : Decimal;
      currencyCode@301480005 : Code[10];
      hasSalesLines@301480006 : Boolean;
      hasPromotions@301480012 : Boolean;
      hasBuergelInvoiceCheck@301480014 : Boolean;
      hasBuergelDeliveryCheck@301480015 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //01 25.05.22 SKU ERP-504 ++

      pSalesHeaderToExport.GET(pSalesHeaderToExport."Document Type"::Order,OSL."Order No");
      IF Contact.GET(pSalesHeaderToExport."Sell-to Contact No.") THEN;

      JSONManagement.InitializeEmptyObject;
      JSONManagement.GetJSONObject(JObject);
      JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'orderNo',pSalesHeaderToExport."No.");
      //JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'orderNo','S69613448378');
      JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'email',Contact."E-Mail");
      //"Order Status":= FORMAT(pSalesHeaderToExport."Order Status");
      JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'status',FORMAT(OSL."Order Status"));
      //JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'datetime',CREATEDATETIME(TODAY,TIME));
      Position := STRPOS(pSalesHeaderToExport."Sell-to Customer Name", ' ');
      IF Position > 0 THEN
        BEGIN
         FirstName := DELSTR(pSalesHeaderToExport."Sell-to Customer Name", Position, STRLEN(pSalesHeaderToExport."Sell-to Customer Name") - Position);
         LastName := DELSTR(pSalesHeaderToExport."Sell-to Customer Name", 1, Position - 1);

        END;
      JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'firstName',FirstName);
      JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'lastName',LastName);

      IF Salu.GET(Contact."Salutation Code") THEN
      //JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'gender',Salu.Description);
      JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'gender',LOWERCASE(Salu.Code));


      IF Contact."Language Code"='ENU' THEN
      LangCode:= 'en';
      IF Contact."Language Code"='DEU'THEN
        LangCode:= 'de';
      IF Contact."Language Code"='NLD'THEN
        LangCode:= 'nl';
      IF Contact."Language Code"='ITA'THEN
        LangCode:= 'it';
      IF Contact."Language Code"='FRA'THEN
        LangCode:= 'fr';
      IF LangCode='' THEN
        LangCode:= 'en';

      JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'language',LangCode);
      IF OSL."Order Status"=OSL."Order Status"::"Shipment In Process" THEN
        GenerateTrackingURL(OSL."Order No");
      JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'link',TrackingURL);

      IF OSL."Order Status"=OSL."Order Status"::"Order Cancelled" THEN BEGIN
      EVALUATE(ReasonCode,OSL."Current Status");
      IF ReasonCode IN [1,2,3,4,5,6,9,12,13,14,17,18,20,21,22,23,24,153] THEN
        cancellationReason:= cancellationReason::"customer request to cancel";
      IF ReasonCode IN [7,8,10,15,25,26] THEN
        cancellationReason:= cancellationReason::"CXT cannot fulfil order";
      IF ReasonCode IN [11,16,19,555] THEN
        cancellationReason:= cancellationReason::"high level that order will be cancelled";
      JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'cancellationReason',FORMAT(cancellationReason));
      END;

      IF OSL."Order Status"=OSL."Order Status"::"Payment Delayed" THEN BEGIN
        IF pSalesHeaderToExport."Currency Code" = '' THEN
          JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'currency','EUR')
        ELSE
          JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'currency',pSalesHeaderToExport."Currency Code");
        //pSalesHeaderToExport.CALCFIELDS(pSalesHeaderToExport."Pre-Pay Amount (expected)");
        JSONManagement.AddJPropertyToJObject(JObject,'amount',pSalesHeaderToExport."Pre-Pay Amount (expected)"-pSalesHeaderToExport."Pre-Pay Amount (found)");

      END;

      JSONManagement.GetJSONObject(JObject);
      EXIT(JObject.ToString());

      //01 25.05.22 SKU ERP-504 --
    END;

    PROCEDURE DoExportSalesOrderStatusToExtInterface@301510018(pExtOrderNo@301480000 : Code[35];pExtInterfaceMessageCode@301480008 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      SalesHeaderToExport@301480006 : Record 36;
      ExtInterfaceOutboundFile@301480009 : Record 5275378;
      ExtInterfaceSetup@301510000 : Record 5275373;
      ExportSalesOrderGateway@301480004 : Codeunit 82013;
      string@301480003 : DotNet "'mscorlib, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b77a5c561934e089'.System.String";
      oStream@301480002 : OutStream;
      xmlText@301480001 : Text;
      OrderExportFailed@301480007 : TextConst 'DEU="Auftrag konnte nicht an das Gateway Åbermittelt werden. AktivitÑtslog ÅberprÅfen. "';
    BEGIN
      //01 25.05.22 SKU ERP-504 ++
      ExtInterfaceSetup.RESET;
      ExtInterfaceSetup.SETFILTER("File Code",pExtInterfaceMessageCode);
      IF ExtInterfaceSetup.FINDFIRST THEN
        IF NOT ExtInterfaceSetup.Active THEN
          EXIT;
      // 01 03.05.21 KEGG MSD-15546 Clean Up External Interface ENDE
      SalesHeaderToExport.RESET;
      SalesHeaderToExport.SETRANGE("External Document No.",pExtOrderNo);
      IF NOT SalesHeaderToExport.FINDFIRST THEN
        EXIT;
      SalesHeaderToExport.SETRECFILTER;

      ExtInterfaceOutboundFile.SourceFile.CREATEOUTSTREAM(oStream,TEXTENCODING::UTF8);
      oStream.WRITE(CreateJsonObjectOrderStatus(Rec));

      ExtInterfaceOutboundFile."Message Code" := pExtInterfaceMessageCode;
      ExtInterfaceOutboundFile.Filename := pExtOrderNo;
      ExtInterfaceOutboundFile.Status := ExtInterfaceOutboundFile.Status::"to process";
      ExtInterfaceOutboundFile.INSERT(TRUE);
      //01 25.05.22 SKU ERP-504 --
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GenerateTrackingURL@301510001(OrderNo@301510000 : Code[30]);
    BEGIN
      TrackingURL:='';
      PackingHead.RESET;
      PackingHead.SETRANGE("Source No.","Order No");
      IF PackingHead.FINDFIRST THEN
        IF PackingHead."Identification Code"<> '' THEN BEGIN
      IF PackingHead."Shipping Agent Code"='FEDEX' THEN
        TrackingURL:= Fedex+PackingHead."Identification Code";

      IF PackingHead."Shipping Agent Code"='FREMD-LOG' THEN BEGIN
        CASE PackingHead."Shipping Agent Service Code" OF
          'OPC':TrackingURL:= OPC+PackingHead."Identification Code";
          'Ferrari':TrackingURL:= Ferrari+PackingHead."Identification Code";
          'DHL-EXPRES':TrackingURL:= STRSUBSTNO(DHLExpress,PackingHead."Identification Code");
          'DHL-EXP' :TrackingURL:= STRSUBSTNO(DHLExpress,PackingHead."Identification Code");
        END;
      END;

      //  IF PackingHead."Shipping Agent Service Code"='OPC' THEN
      //  TrackingURL:= OPC+PackingHead."Identification Code"
      //  ELSE BEGIN
      //  TrackingURL:= Ferrari+PackingHead."Identification Code";

      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      ITBO-1197
    }
    END.
  }
}

    PROCEDURE DoRegisterCode@610800000();
    VAR
      CreditRankingCode_CXT@301510000 : Record 50014;
    BEGIN
      // NF 01 02.08.18 CZIE CHR-56 OnRegister (Publisher) | RegisterCode
      CreditRankingCode_CXT.RESET;
      CreditRankingCode_CXT.SETRANGE("Service Provider","Service Provider");
      CreditRankingCode_CXT.SETRANGE(Code,Code);
      IF NOT CreditRankingCode_CXT.ISEMPTY THEN
        EXIT;

      CreditRankingCode_CXT.TRANSFERFIELDS(Rec);
      CreditRankingCode_CXT.INSERT(TRUE);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE FindSerialNo@301510002();
    VAR
      ile@301510000 : Record 32;
    BEGIN
      ile.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
      ile.SETRANGE("Variant Code","Variant Code");
      ile.SETFILTER("Remaining Quantity",'>=%1',1);
      IF ile.FINDLAST THEN
        "Serial No.":=ile."Serial No.";
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      // 01 22.09.22 SKU ERP-665 new table for photoshoot Entry
    }
    END.
  }

    LOCAL PROCEDURE InitVendorNo@301510000();
    VAR
      VendorIntegration@301510001 : Record 50029;
      LastNunber@301510000 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      IF VendorIntegration.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
        VendorId:=  'V001';
        "Vendor Identifier":= '001';
      END ELSE BEGIN
        VendorIntegration.FINDLAST;
        EVALUATE(LastNunber,COPYSTR(VendorIntegration.VendorId,2,4));
        IF LastNunber = 999 THEN
          ERROR('Max vendors reached');
        CASE STRLEN(FORMAT(LastNunber)) OF
          1:BEGIN
            VendorId:=  'V00' + FORMAT(LastNunber + 1);
            "Vendor Identifier":=  '00' + FORMAT(LastNunber + 1);
          END;
          2:BEGIN
            IF LastNunber = 99 THEN BEGIN
              VendorId:=  'V' + FORMAT(LastNunber + 1);
              "Vendor Identifier":= FORMAT(LastNunber + 1);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              VendorId:=  'V0' + FORMAT(LastNunber + 1);
              "Vendor Identifier":=  '0' + FORMAT(LastNunber + 1);
            END;
          END;
          3:BEGIN
              VendorId:=  'V' + FORMAT(LastNunber + 1);
              "Vendor Identifier":= FORMAT(LastNunber + 1);
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE NoSeriesInsert@301510003();
    VAR
      NoSeries@301510000 : Record 308;
      NoSeriesLine@301510001 : Record 309;
    BEGIN
      IF NoSeries.GET THEN
        ERROR('No Series Exist');

      NoSeries.INIT;
      NoSeries.Code :=  VendorId;
      NoSeries.Description    := 'marketPlace Variant No Series for ' + VendorId;
      NoSeries."Default Nos." := TRUE;
      NoSeries."Manual Nos."  := FALSE;
      NoSeries."Date Order"   := TRUE;
      NoSeries.INSERT(TRUE);

      NoSeriesLine.INIT;
      NoSeriesLine."Series Code"  := VendorId;
      NoSeriesLine."Line No."     := 10000;
      NoSeriesLine."Starting No." := VendorId+'00001';
      NoSeriesLine."Ending No."   := VendorId+'99999';
      NoSeriesLine.Open           := TRUE;
      IF NoSeriesLine.INSERT(TRUE) THEN;

      "Variant Nos." := VendorId;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE TestSetup@301510001();
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD("Buy-from Vendor No.");
      TESTFIELD("Standard delivery time");

      //ITBO-779 Start
      //TESTFIELD("Actual Cost %");
      IF "Additional Sales Price %"  = 0 THEN
        TESTFIELD("Actual Cost %");

      IF "Actual Cost %"  = 0 THEN
        TESTFIELD("Additional Sales Price %");
      //ITBO-779 End

      TESTFIELD("Product Postings Group ID Code");
      TESTFIELD("Gen. Prod. Posting Group");
      TESTFIELD("VAT Prod. Posting Group");
      TESTFIELD("Inventory Posting Group");
      TESTFIELD("Variant Nos.");
      TESTFIELD("Buyer Country Tax%");
      TESTFIELD("Seller Country Tax%");
      TESTFIELD("Buyer Country Tax%");
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckUserLevelRestriction@301510002();
    BEGIN
      IF NOT ((USERID = 'SGALLIPELLI') OR (USERID = 'ATRINCZEK') OR (USERID = 'SKUMAR') OR (USERID = 'JHAHN') OR (USERID = 'PONGKOWIDJAJA') OR (USERID = 'ESCHLEUSSINGER') //IT team
         OR (USERID = 'PWEINER') OR (USERID = 'ASHARMA') OR (USERID = 'PSCHOMAKER') OR (USERID = 'MOKTAY') ) THEN //Business team
        ERROR('Not allowed');
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      // 00 14.11.22 SGA ERP-657 Consignment Sales(LVI1.0)
      // 01 10.01.23 SGA ITBO-749 Consignment configuration with Prepayment
      // 01 17.02.23 SGA ITBO-779 Consignment configuration with no cost
      // 01 13.04.23 SGA ITBO-842 Update Standard Delivery Time for LV Watches
    }
    END.
  }
}

    VAR
      B2B2CMarketPlaceSetup@301510000 : Record 50029;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetFieldCaption@31(FieldNumber@1000 : Integer) : Text[100];
    VAR
      ItemAttribute@301480000 : Record 7500;
    BEGIN

      IF ItemAttribute.GET(FieldNumber) THEN
        EXIT(ItemAttribute.Name)
      ELSE
        EXIT(GetFieldName((FieldNumber)));
    END;

    [External]
    PROCEDURE GetCaptionClass@34(FieldNumber@1000 : Integer) : Text[80];
    VAR
      SalesHeader2@1565 : Record 36;
    BEGIN
      EXIT(STRSUBSTNO('%1',GetFieldCaption(FieldNumber)));
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetFieldName@301480000(FieldNumber@301480000 : Integer) : Text[80];
    VAR
      Field@301480001 : Record 2000000041;
    BEGIN
      IF Field.GET(DATABASE::"Item Attribute Collection",FieldNumber) THEN
        EXIT(Field.FieldName);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckVendorIsActive@301510000();
    BEGIN
      IF NOT B2B2CMarketPlaceSetup.GET(VendorId) THEN
        ERROR('Invalid Vendor');

      IF NOT B2B2CMarketPlaceSetup.Active THEN
        ERROR('Vendor is not active');
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckMandatoryAttributes@301510001();
    VAR
      Fref@301510000 : FieldRef;
    BEGIN
      IF Price =  0 THEN
        ERROR('price should not be zero');

      IF Reference = '' THEN
        ShowBlankvalueError('itemAttributeId_5');

      IF "Vendor Item Id" = '' THEN
        ShowBlankvalueError('vendorItemId');

      {
      IF Year = 0 THEN
        ShowBlankvalueError('Year');
      }
      IF Taxation = '' THEN
        ShowBlankvalueError('itemAttributeId_12');
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckSourceIdExist@301510003();
    VAR
      ItemVendor@301510000 : Record 99;
      ConsignmentMarketPlaceItems@301510001 : Record 50030;
    BEGIN
      ConsignmentMarketPlaceItems.SETRANGE("Vendor Item Id",Rec."Vendor Item Id");
      IF NOT ConsignmentMarketPlaceItems.ISEMPTY THEN
        ERROR(Rec."Vendor Item Id" + ' already exist');
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ShowBlankvalueError@301510002(FieldName@301510000 : Text);
    BEGIN
      ERROR(FieldName + ' is blank');
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      // 00 14.11.22 SGA ERP-657 Consignment Sales(LVI1.0)
      // 01 17.01.23 SGA ERP-733 Email Notifications from Diva
    }
    END.
  }
}

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetFieldCaption@31(FieldNumber@1000 : Integer) : Text[100];
    VAR
      ItemAttribute@301480000 : Record 7500;
    BEGIN
      IF ItemAttribute.GET(FieldNumber) THEN
        EXIT(ItemAttribute.Name)
      ELSE
        EXIT(GetFieldName((FieldNumber)));
    END;

    [External]
    PROCEDURE GetCaptionClass@34(FieldNumber@1000 : Integer) : Text[80];
    VAR
      SalesHeader2@1565 : Record 36;
    BEGIN
      EXIT(STRSUBSTNO('%1',GetFieldCaption(FieldNumber)));
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetFieldName@301480000(FieldNumber@301480000 : Integer) : Text[80];
    VAR
      Field@301480001 : Record 2000000041;
    BEGIN
      IF Field.GET(DATABASE::"Item Attribute Collection",FieldNumber) THEN
        EXIT(Field.FieldName);
    END;

    BEGIN
    END.

               ENU=Item-Function-Mapping];

    { 1   ;   ;Item No.            ;Code20        ;DataClassification=ToBeClassified;
                                                   CaptionML=[DEU=Artikelnr.;

    { 2   ;   ;Function Id         ;Code20        ;DataClassification=ToBeClassified;
                                                   CaptionML=[DEU=Funktions-ID;
                                                              ENU=Function-ID] }
    { 3   ;   ;Description         ;Text250       ;FieldClass=FlowField;
                                                   CalcFormula=Lookup("System Value Line".Description WHERE (System Value No.=CONST(85000),
                                                                                                             System Code=FIELD(Function Id)));
                                                   CaptionML=[DEU=Beschreibung;
                                                              ENU=Description] }
    { 4   ;   ;Assigned            ;Boolean       ;OnValidate=VAR
                                                                lItemFunctionMapping@301480000 : Record 85000;
                                                                lSystemValueLine@301480001 : Record 5105031;
                                                              BEGIN
                                                                IF NOT Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
                                                                  EXIT;

                                                                IF NOT lItemFunctionMapping.GET(Rec."Item No.",Rec."Function Id") THEN BEGIN
                                                                  lItemFunctionMapping.INIT;
                                                                  lItemFunctionMapping."Item No." := Rec."Item No.";
                                                                  lItemFunctionMapping."Function Id" := Rec."Function Id";
                                                                  lItemFunctionMapping.INSERT;

                                                                  lSystemValueLine.RESET;
                                                                  lSystemValueLine.SETRANGE("System Value No.", 85000);
                                                                  lSystemValueLine.SETRANGE("System Code", lItemFunctionMapping."Function Id");
                                                                  IF lSystemValueLine.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                                                                    lSystemValueLine."Bool 1" := TRUE;
                                                                    lSystemValueLine.MODIFY;
                                                                  END;

                                                                END ELSE BEGIN
                                                                  lItemFunctionMapping.DELETE;
                                                                  lItemFunctionMapping.RESET;
                                                                  lItemFunctionMapping.SETRANGE("Function Id", Rec."Function Id");
                                                                  IF lItemFunctionMapping.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
                                                                    lSystemValueLine.RESET;
                                                                    lSystemValueLine.SETRANGE("System Value No.", 85000);
                                                                    lSystemValueLine.SETRANGE("System Code", lItemFunctionMapping."Function Id");
                                                                    IF lSystemValueLine.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                                                                      lSystemValueLine."Bool 1" := FALSE;
                                                                      lSystemValueLine.MODIFY;
                                                                    END;
                                                                  END;
                                                                END;
                                                              END;

                                                   DataClassification=ToBeClassified;
                                                   CaptionML=[DEU=Zugeordnet;
                                                              ENU=Assigned] }
    { 5   ;   ;Sorting             ;Integer       ;DataClassification=ToBeClassified;
                                                   CaptionML=[DEU=Sorting;
                                                              ENU=Sorting] }
  }

    PROCEDURE GetDescriptionInCurrentLanguage@301480001() : Text;
    VAR
      lSystemValueManagement@301480000 : Codeunit 5105030;
      lSystemValueLine@301480001 : Record 5105031;
    BEGIN
      IF lSystemValueLine.GET(85000,"Function Id") THEN
        EXIT(lSystemValueManagement.TranslateSystemValueLine_WindowsLanguageID(lSystemValueLine,WINDOWSLANGUAGE,3));
    END;

    PROCEDURE LoadItemFunctionsFactBoxData@301480000(pItemNo@301480000 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      lItemFunctionMapping@301480001 : Record 85000;
      lSystemValueLine@301480002 : Record 5105031;
      lSort@301490000 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      IF NOT Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      RESET;
      DELETEALL;
      lItemFunctionMapping.SETRANGE("Item No.",pItemNo);
      // 01 15.07.19 LACK CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Sortierung
      lItemFunctionMapping.SETCURRENTKEY(Description);
      lSort := 1;
      // 01 15.07.19 LACK CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Sortierung Ende
      IF lItemFunctionMapping.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          IF lSystemValueLine.GET(85000,lItemFunctionMapping."Function Id") THEN BEGIN
            TRANSFERFIELDS(lItemFunctionMapping);
            // 01 15.07.19 LACK CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Sortierung
            Sorting := lSort;
            lSort += 1;
            // 01 15.07.19 LACK CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Sortierung Ende
            INSERT;
          END
        UNTIL lItemFunctionMapping.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE LoadItemFunctionsEditorData@301480002(pItemNo@301480000 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      lSystemValueLine@301480001 : Record 5105031;
      lItemFunctionMapping@301480002 : Record 85000;
      lSort@301490000 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      IF NOT Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      RESET;
      DELETEALL;
      lSystemValueLine.SETRANGE("System Value No.",85000);
      // 01 15.07.19 LACK CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Sortierung
      lSort :=1;
      lSystemValueLine.SETCURRENTKEY(Description);
      // 01 15.07.19 LACK CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Sortierung Ende
      IF lSystemValueLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          Rec.INIT;
          Rec."Item No." := pItemNo;
          Rec."Function Id" := lSystemValueLine."System Code";

          Rec.Sorting := lSort;
          lSort +=1;
          // 01 15.07.19 LACK CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Sortierung
          Rec.INSERT;
        UNTIL lSystemValueLine.NEXT = 0;

      lItemFunctionMapping.SETRANGE("Item No.",pItemNo);
      IF lItemFunctionMapping.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          IF Rec.GET(lItemFunctionMapping."Item No.", lItemFunctionMapping."Function Id") THEN BEGIN
            Rec.Assigned := TRUE;
            Rec.MODIFY;
          END;
        UNTIL lItemFunctionMapping.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      // 00 25.07.18 MKLE CHR-48 Added Table Item Function Mapping
      // NF 15.07.19 LACK CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Sorting
      // 01 15.07.19 LACK CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Sortierung
    }
    END.
  }
}

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetFieldCaption@31(FieldNumber@1000 : Integer) : Text[100];
    VAR
      ItemAttribute@301480000 : Record 7500;
    BEGIN
      IF ItemAttribute.GET(FieldNumber) THEN
        EXIT(ItemAttribute.Name)
      ELSE
        EXIT(GetFieldName((FieldNumber)));
    END;

    [External]
    PROCEDURE GetCaptionClass@34(FieldNumber@1000 : Integer) : Text[80];
    VAR
      SalesHeader2@1565 : Record 36;
    BEGIN
      EXIT(STRSUBSTNO('%1',GetFieldCaption(FieldNumber)));
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetFieldName@301480000(FieldNumber@301480000 : Integer) : Text[80];
    VAR
      Field@301480001 : Record 2000000041;
    BEGIN
      IF Field.GET(DATABASE::"Item Attribute Collection",FieldNumber) THEN
        EXIT(Field.FieldName);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      // 00 01.04.19 MBLA CHR-248 Erweiterte Artikelsuche ist nicht nutzbar aufgrund mangelnder Performance
      // DF 24.05.19 MKLE CHR-548 48 <Hersteller-Seriennr.>
      // DF 24.05.19 MKLE CHR-548 49 <Garantiedatum>
      // 01 28.05.19 LACK CHR-548 Datetime fÅr GeÑndert Am / EingefÅgt am
      // 01 26.06.19 MBLA CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Modell soll nicht klickbar sein
      // 01 02.07.19 MBLA CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: <Erfa·t am/durch> ersetzt mit <Angelegt am/durch>
      // UP 03.07.19 MBLA CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: BlankZero = Yes bei allen Integer und Decimal Feldern
      // 01 15.07.19 LACK CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Captions angepasst
      // 01 15.07.19 LACK CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Referenz Text LÑnge
      // NF 29.07.19 MBLA CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: <Hersteller-Seriennr.>
      // NF 29.07.19 MBLA CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: <Hersteller-Seriennr.>
      // GF 31.07.19 MBLA CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: <GetCaptionClass>
      // LF 31.07.19 MBLA CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: <GetFieldName>
      // LF 31.07.19 MBLA CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: <GetFieldCaption>
      // UF 31.07.19 MBLA CHR-548 Anforderungen Artikel / Varianten: Div. FeldlÑngen angepasst. Max. ZeichenlÑnge ermittelt:
        SELECT [Attribute ID],MAX(Value),LEN(MAX(Value)) FROM [dbo].[CHRONEXT-GmbH$Item Attribute Value]
        GROUP BY [Attribute ID]
        HAVING LEN(MAX(Value)) > 30
      // 01 28.02.20 LACk MSD-11069 Fertigstellung Artikel-Anlage
      // NF 11.09.20 TIST MSD-12853 Feldanpassungen 87 NOS(Never out of stock) & 88 ZZ Ziffernblatt
      // 01 12.11.20 VMO MSD-13690 Add field 36 Popup Shop
      // 01 22.12.20 TIST MSD-14165 Feldanpassung - 49 Garantiedatum - wieder hinzugefÅgt
      // 01 09.02.21 VMO ERP-201 Add field 89 Shop Status
      // 01 15.02.22 SKU ERP-464 Add field Copied
      // 01 16.03.22 SGA ERP-512 Added new attribute "Publisher Date" for webshop
    }
    END.
  }

            END ELSE BEGIN

    PROCEDURE RefillNavisionFields@301490021(pSettlementRegister@301490001 : Record 85012;pOnlySetupInsert@301490003 : Boolean);
    VAR
      SettlementRegister1@301490002 : Record 85012;
      SettlementRegister3@301490000 : Record 85012;
    BEGIN
      // 01 22.01.21 HBIE ERP-93 CR Erweiterung auf Datei-Option "sortenrein"

      //Kopfsatz ermitteln
      SettlementRegister3.RESET;
      SettlementRegister3.SETCURRENTKEY("Import No.","Entry No.");
      SettlementRegister3.SETRANGE("Import No.",pSettlementRegister."Import No.");
      SettlementRegister3.SETFILTER(SettlementRegister3."Entry No.",'<=%1',pSettlementRegister."Entry No.");
      SettlementRegister3.SETRANGE("Entry Type",SettlementRegister3."Entry Type"::Register);
      SettlementRegister3.FIND('-');

      //Kopfsatz zurÅcksetzen
      WITH SettlementRegister3 DO BEGIN
        CLEAR("Payment Method Code");
        CLEAR("Payment Method Code");
        CLEAR("Transaction Date");
        CLEAR("Gross Currency Code");
        CLEAR("Gross Amount");
        CLEAR("Curr. Exchange Rate");
        CLEAR("Net Currency Code");
        CLEAR(Net);
        CLEAR(Commission);
        CLEAR(Markup);
        CLEAR("Scheme Fee");
        CLEAR(Interchange);
        CLEAR("Account Type");
        CLEAR("Account No.");
        CLEAR("Sign Option");
        CLEAR("Net Amount");
        CLEAR("Net Curr. Code");
        CLEAR("Markup Amount");
        CLEAR("Scheme Fee Amount");
        CLEAR("Interchange Amount");
        CLEAR("Exch. Rate");
        CLEAR("Net Payment Amount");
        CLEAR("Net Refund Amount");
        CLEAR("Commission Amount");
        CLEAR("Fee Amount");
        CLEAR("Reserve Balance Amount");
      END;

      //Zeilen abarbeiten
      SettlementRegister1.RESET;
      SettlementRegister1.SETRANGE("Entry No.",SettlementRegister3."Entry No." + 1,SettlementRegister3."Entry No." + SettlementRegister3."No. of Entries");
      IF NOT SettlementRegister1.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
        //Dateityp ermitteln
        SettlementRegister1.SETFILTER("Exchange Rate",'<>%1&<>%2','','1');
        IF SettlementRegister1.ISEMPTY THEN
          SettlementRegister3."File Type" := SettlementRegister3."File Type"::"Curr.-separated"
        ELSE
          SettlementRegister3."File Type" := SettlementRegister3."File Type"::EUR;
        SettlementRegister1.SETRANGE("Exchange Rate");
        SettlementRegister1.FIND('-');
        REPEAT
          //NAV-Felder fÅllen
          SettlementRegister1.TESTFIELD("Import No.",SettlementRegister3."Import No.");
          SettlementRegister1.TESTFIELD("Entry Type",SettlementRegister1."Entry Type"::Entry);
          FillNavisionFields(SettlementRegister3,SettlementRegister1,pOnlySetupInsert);
        UNTIL SettlementRegister1.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      //Setup prÅfen
      gTEMPAdyenSetup.RESET;
      gTEMPAdyenSetup.SETRANGE("Check Ok",FALSE);
      IF pOnlySetupInsert THEN BEGIN
        IF gTEMPAdyenSetup.ISEMPTY THEN
          MESSAGE('Einrichtung ist Ok')
        ELSE
          MESSAGE('Bitte Setup prÅfen');
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF NOT gTEMPAdyenSetup.ISEMPTY THEN
          ERROR('Bitte Setup prÅfen.\NAV-Felder wurden nicht gefÅllt');
      END;

      COMMIT;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE FillNavisionFields@301490000(VAR SettlementRegister3@301490002 : Record 85012;VAR SettlementRegister1@301490001 : Record 85012;pOnlySetupInsert@301490000 : Boolean);
    BEGIN
      // 01 22.01.21 HBIE ERP-93 CR Erweiterung auf Datei-Option "sortenrein"

      //SettlementRegister3 = Kopf, SettlementRegister1 = Zeile
      //Programmcode aus Funktion kopiert und angepasst, Kommentare entfernt

      //NAV-Felder fÅllen
      //dazu Einrichtungen in Systemwert lesen / ergÑnzen

      WITH SettlementRegister1 DO BEGIN

        "read/insert Adyen Setup"("Company Account","Merchant Account",'','','Buchblatt, Konten');
        "read/insert Adyen Setup"("Company Account","Merchant Account","Payment Method",'','Zahlungsform');
        "read/insert Adyen Setup"("Company Account","Merchant Account","Payment Method",Type,'Buchungstyp');

        IF NOT pOnlySetupInsert THEN BEGIN
          gTEMPAdyenSetup.RESET;
          gTEMPAdyenSetup.SETRANGE("Check Ok",FALSE);
          IF NOT gTEMPAdyenSetup.ISEMPTY THEN
            ERROR('Bitte Setup prÅfen.\Verarbeitung abgebrochen');
        END;

        IF pOnlySetupInsert THEN
          EXIT;

        //Zeilensatz zurÅcksetzen
        CLEAR("Payment Method Code");
        CLEAR("Payment Method Code");
        CLEAR("Transaction Date");
        CLEAR("Gross Currency Code");
        CLEAR("Gross Amount");
        CLEAR("Curr. Exchange Rate");
        CLEAR("Net Currency Code");
        CLEAR(Net);
        CLEAR(Commission);
        CLEAR(Markup);
        CLEAR("Scheme Fee");
        CLEAR(Interchange);
        CLEAR("Account Type");
        CLEAR("Account No.");
        CLEAR("Sign Option");
        CLEAR("Net Amount");
        CLEAR("Net Curr. Code");
        CLEAR(Markup);
        CLEAR("Scheme Fee");
        CLEAR(Interchange);
        CLEAR("Exch. Rate");
        CLEAR("Net Payment Amount");
        CLEAR("Net Refund Amount");
        CLEAR("Commission Amount");
        CLEAR("Fee Amount");
        CLEAR("Reserve Balance Amount");
        CLEAR("Account Type");
        CLEAR("Account No.");
        "read/insert Adyen Setup"("Company Account","Merchant Account",'','','Buchblatt, Konten');
        "read/insert Adyen Setup"("Company Account","Merchant Account","Payment Method",'','Zahlungsform');
        "read/insert Adyen Setup"("Company Account","Merchant Account","Payment Method",Type,'Buchungstyp');

        gTEMPAdyenSetup.GET("Company Account","Merchant Account","Payment Method",'');
        "Payment Method Code" := gTEMPAdyenSetup."Payment Method Code";

        gTEMPAdyenSetup.GET("Company Account","Merchant Account","Payment Method",Type);

        "Transaction Date" := getDateOfTimestamp("Creation Date Orig.");
        "Gross Currency Code" := UPPERCASE("Gross Currency");
        "Net Currency Code" := UPPERCASE("Net Currency");
        "Curr. Exchange Rate" := evaluateDecimal("Exchange Rate",'');

        Commission := evaluateDecimal("Commission (NC)",'');
        Markup := evaluateDecimal("Markup (NC)",'');
        "Scheme Fee" := evaluateDecimal("Scheme Fees (NC)",'');
        Interchange := evaluateDecimal("Interchange (NC)",'');

        IF NOT pOnlySetupInsert THEN BEGIN
          Net := evaluateDecimal("Net Credit (NC)","Net Debit (NC)");
          CASE gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type" OF

            gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type"::" ": gTEMPAdyenSetup.TESTFIELD("Posting Type");

            gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type"::Ignore: ;

            gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type"::Payment,gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type"::Refund:
              BEGIN //Zahlungen und Erstattungen wie bisher
                "Gross Amount" := evaluateDecimal("Gross Credit (GC)","Gross Debit (GC)");
                //Net := evaluateDecimal("Net Credit (NC)","Net Debit (NC)");
              END;

            gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type"::Fee:
              BEGIN //neu
                "Fee Amount" := -evaluateDecimal("Net Credit (NC)","Net Debit (NC)");
              END;

            gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type"::Reserve:
              BEGIN //neu
                "Reserve Balance Amount" := -evaluateDecimal("Net Credit (NC)","Net Debit (NC)");
              END;

            gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type"::"Account Posting":
              BEGIN
                "Account Type" := gTEMPAdyenSetup."Account Type";
                "Account No."  := gTEMPAdyenSetup."Account No.";
                "Sign Option"  := gTEMPAdyenSetup."Sign Option";
                IF NOT pOnlySetupInsert THEN
                  TESTFIELD("Account No.");
                //"Gross Amount" := evaluateDecimal("Gross Credit (GC)","Gross Debit (GC)");
                //Net := evaluateDecimal("Net Credit (NC)","Net Debit (NC)");
              END;
          END;
        END;

        IF NOT pOnlySetupInsert THEN
          MODIFY;

        //SettlementRegister3."To Entry No" := "Entry No.";
        //SettlementRegister3."No. of Entries" += 1;
        IF NOT (gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type" = gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type"::Ignore) THEN BEGIN
          CASE gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type" OF
            gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type"::Payment:
              SettlementRegister3."Net Payment Amount" += Net;
            gTEMPAdyenSetup."Posting Type"::Refund:
              SettlementRegister3."Net Refund Amount" -= Net;
          END;
          SettlementRegister3."Curr. Code" := "Gross Currency Code";
          SettlementRegister3."Net Amount" += Net;
          IF SettlementRegister3."File Type" = SettlementRegister3."File Type"::EUR THEN
            SettlementRegister3."Net Curr. Code" := "Net Currency Code";

          // 01 22.01.21 HBIE ERP-93 CR Erweiterung auf Datei-Option "sortenrein"
          IF SettlementRegister3."File Type" = SettlementRegister3."File Type"::"Curr.-separated" THEN BEGIN
            "Commission Amount" := Commission;
            "Markup Amount" := Markup;
            "Scheme Fee Amount" := "Scheme Fee";
            "Interchange Amount" := Interchange;
            "Fee Amount" := "Fee Amount";
            "Reserve Balance Amount" := "Reserve Balance Amount";
            "Exch. Rate" := "Curr. Exchange Rate";
          END ELSE BEGIN
          // 01 22.01.21 HBIE ERP-93 CR Erweiterung auf Datei-Option "sortenrein" Ende
            SettlementRegister3."Commission Amount" += Commission;
            SettlementRegister3."Markup Amount" += Markup;
            SettlementRegister3."Scheme Fee Amount" += "Scheme Fee";
            SettlementRegister3."Interchange Amount" += Interchange;
            SettlementRegister3."Fee Amount" += "Fee Amount";
            SettlementRegister3."Reserve Balance Amount" += "Reserve Balance Amount";
            SettlementRegister3."Exch. Rate" := "Curr. Exchange Rate";
          END;// 01 22.01.21 HBIE ERP-93

          IF "Batch Number" > 0 THEN
            SettlementRegister3."Batch Number" := "Batch Number";
          SettlementRegister3.MODIFY;

        END;
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE getNextField@301480001(VAR _ImpText@301480001 : Text[1024];_Chr@301480002 : Text[1];_max_Length@301480000 : Integer) returnText : Text[250];
    BEGIN
      IF STRPOS(_ImpText,_Chr) = 0 THEN BEGIN
        returnText := COPYSTR(_ImpText,1,_max_Length);
        _ImpText := '';
      END ELSE BEGIN
        // 01 15.02.20 HBIE MSD-9678 Import: Feldbebeginn >"< deaktiviert Feldtrenner >,<
        //returnText := COPYSTR(COPYSTR(_ImpText,1,STRPOS(_ImpText,_Chr) -1),1,_max_Length);
        //_ImpText := COPYSTR(_ImpText,STRPOS(_ImpText,_Chr) + 1);
        IF COPYSTR(_ImpText,1,1) = '"' THEN BEGIN
          returnText := COPYSTR(COPYSTR(_ImpText,2,STRPOS(_ImpText,'"' + _Chr) - 2),1,_max_Length);
          _ImpText := COPYSTR(_ImpText,STRPOS(_ImpText,'"' + _Chr) + 2);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          returnText := COPYSTR(COPYSTR(_ImpText,1,STRPOS(_ImpText,_Chr) - 1),1,_max_Length);
          _ImpText := COPYSTR(_ImpText,STRPOS(_ImpText,_Chr) + 1);
        END;
        // 01 15.02.20 HBIE MSD-9678 Import: Feldbebeginn >"< deaktiviert Feldtrenner >,< Ende
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE getNextIntField@301480002(VAR _ImpText@301480003 : Text[1024];_Chr@301480002 : Text[1];max_Length@301480000 : Integer) returnInt : Integer;
    VAR
      tempText@301480001 : Text[30];
    BEGIN
      tempText := getNextField(_ImpText,_Chr,max_Length);
      IF NOT EVALUATE(returnInt,tempText) THEN
        returnInt := 0;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE evaluateDecimal@301480003(pDebitText@301480000 : Text[30];PCreditText@301480001 : Text[30]) rDecimal : Decimal;
    VAR
      tempTxt@301480004 : Text[30];
      tempTxt2@301480005 : Text[30];
      tempInt@301480002 : Integer;
      sign_neg@301480003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      rDecimal := 0;
      sign_neg := FALSE;
      pDebitText := DELCHR(pDebitText,'<>',' ');
      PCreditText := DELCHR(PCreditText,'<>',' ');
      CASE TRUE OF
        (pDebitText = '') AND (PCreditText = ''): EXIT;
        (pDebitText <> '') AND (PCreditText <> ''): EXIT;
        PCreditText = '': tempTxt := pDebitText;
        pDebitText = '': BEGIN
                           tempTxt := PCreditText;
                           sign_neg := TRUE;
                         END;
      END;

      tempTxt2 := '';
      IF STRPOS(tempTxt,'.') > 0 THEN BEGIN
        tempTxt2 := ',' + COPYSTR(tempTxt,STRPOS(tempTxt,'.') + 1);
        tempTxt := COPYSTR(tempTxt,1,STRPOS(tempTxt,'.') - 1);
      END;

      IF NOT EVALUATE(rDecimal,tempTxt + tempTxt2) THEN
        rDecimal := 0
      ELSE BEGIN
        IF sign_neg THEN
          rDecimal := - rDecimal;
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE getDateOfTimestamp@301480010(pDateTimeText@301480000 : Text[30]) rDate : Date;
    BEGIN
      pDateTimeText := COPYSTR(pDateTimeText,9,2) + '.' +
                       COPYSTR(pDateTimeText,6,2) + '.' +
                       COPYSTR(pDateTimeText,3,2);
      IF NOT EVALUATE(rDate,pDateTimeText) THEN
        rDate := 0D;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE "read/insert Adyen Setup"@301480000(pCompanyAccount@301480000 : Text;pMerchantAccount@301480001 : Text;pPaymentMethod@301480002 : Text;pType@301480003 : Text;pDesciption@301480004 : Text[30]);
    VAR
      AdyenSetup@301480005 : Record 85013;
    BEGIN
      IF (pCompanyAccount = '') AND (pMerchantAccount = '') THEN
        EXIT;
      IF NOT gTEMPAdyenSetup.GET(pCompanyAccount,pMerchantAccount,pPaymentMethod,pType) THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT AdyenSetup.GET(pCompanyAccount,pMerchantAccount,pPaymentMethod,pType) THEN BEGIN
          AdyenSetup.INIT;
          AdyenSetup."Company Account" := pCompanyAccount;
          AdyenSetup."Merchant Account" := pMerchantAccount;
          AdyenSetup."Payment Method" := pPaymentMethod;
          AdyenSetup.Type := pType;
          AdyenSetup."Check Ok" := FALSE;
          AdyenSetup.Description := pDesciption;
          AdyenSetup.INSERT;
        END;
        gTEMPAdyenSetup.TRANSFERFIELDS(AdyenSetup);
        gTEMPAdyenSetup.INSERT;
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      // 00 24.12.19 HBIE MSD-9678 Objekt angelegt
      // 01 15.02.20 HBIE MSD-9678 Feld "Modification Reference" von 60 auf 100 Zeichen
      // NF 15.02.20 HBIE MSD-9678 "Fee Amount" "Reserve Balance Amount"
      // 01 15.02.20 HBIE MSD-9678 Import: Feldbebeginn >"< deaktiviert Feldtrenner >,<
      // 01 15.02.20 HBIE MSD-9678 Erweiterung "Fee Amount" "Reserve Balance Amount"
      // NF 10.05.20 HBIE MSD-9678 "Account Type" "Account No." "Sign Option"
      // 01 10.05.20 HBIE MSD-9678 Erweiterung Kreditor-Verrechnung
      // 01 16.05.20 HBIE MSD-9678 Korrekturen
      // 01 18.05.20 HBIE MSD-9678 Erweiterung Sign-Option
      // NF 22.01.21 HBIE ERP-93 "File Type" (Option Field, CR Erweiterung auf Datei-Option "sortenrein")
    }
    END.
  }
}

  CODE
  {
    VAR
      IsRenameAllowed@301490000 : Boolean;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE UpdateVendorNo@1();
    VAR
      Vendor@1000 : Record 23;
    BEGIN
      IF ISNULLGUID("Vendor Id") THEN
        EXIT;

      Vendor.SETRANGE(Id,"Vendor Id");
      IF NOT Vendor.FINDFIRST THEN
        EXIT;

      "Buy-from Vendor No." := Vendor."No.";
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE UpdateVendorId@2();
    VAR
      Vendor@1000 : Record 23;
    BEGIN
      IF "Buy-from Vendor No." = '' THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR("Vendor Id");
        EXIT;
      END;

      IF NOT Vendor.GET("Buy-from Vendor No.") THEN
        EXIT;

      "Vendor Id" := Vendor.Id;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE UpdateOrderNo@4();
    VAR
      PurchaseHeader@1000 : Record 38;
    BEGIN
       IF ISNULLGUID("Order Id") THEN
        EXIT;

       PurchaseHeader.SETRANGE(Id,"Order Id");
       PurchaseHeader.SETRANGE("Document Type",PurchaseHeader."Document Type"::Order);
       IF NOT PurchaseHeader.FINDFIRST THEN
        EXIT;

       "Order No." := PurchaseHeader."No.";
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE UpdateOrderId@3();
    VAR
      PurchaseHeader@1000 : Record 38;
    BEGIN
      IF NOT PurchaseHeader.GET(PurchaseHeader."Document Type"::Order,"Order No.") THEN
        EXIT;

      "Order Id" := PurchaseHeader.Id;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateReferencedRecordIds@5();
    BEGIN
      UpdateVendorId;

      IF ("Order No." <> '') AND ISNULLGUID("Order Id") THEN
        UpdateOrderId;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetIsRenameAllowed@6(RenameAllowed@1000 : Boolean);
    BEGIN
      IsRenameAllowed := RenameAllowed;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      // 00 29.09.18 MBLA INTERNAL Purchase Order Api
    }
    END.
  }
}

    PROCEDURE UpdateItemId@1();
    VAR
      Item@1000 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      IF ("No." = '') OR (Type <> Type::Item) THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR("Item Id");
        EXIT;
      END;

      IF NOT Item.GET("No.") THEN
        EXIT;

      "Item Id" := Item.Id;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateNo@5();
    VAR
      Item@1001 : Record 27;
      GLAccount@1000 : Record 15;
    BEGIN
       CASE Type OF
        Type::Item:
          BEGIN
            Item.SETRANGE(Id,"Item Id");
            IF NOT Item.FINDFIRST THEN
              EXIT;

            "No." := Item."No.";
          END;
      //  Type::"G/L Account":
      //    BEGIN
      //      GLAccount.SETRANGE(Id,"Account Id");
      //      IF NOT GLAccount.FINDFIRST THEN
      //        EXIT;
      //
      //      "No." := GLAccount."No.";
      //    END;
       END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      // 00 29.09.18 MBLA INTERNAL Purchase Order Api
    }
    END.
  }
}
